Content note: The first volume of Walther's paper, September 1844 to 1845. Divisions: fortnightly numbers (No. 1 onward); index pages located separately. Mixed voices: correspondence and reprinted tracts appear in the numbers. Cautions: Not a bound title page: imprint from the first issue date.
